So... yeah.\n\nDoctor: During these recent periods where you felt so heavy and sad, did you ever feel like you were losing touch with reality, such as hearing voices or having thoughts that others found hard to believe?\n\nPatient: No. I mean... no, nothing like that. Just... just the sadness.\n\nDoctor: During that period after the jewelry show when you felt 'wired' and larger than life, did you ever feel like you were having thoughts that were unusual, or did you see or hear things that weren't actually there?\n\nPatient: No. Uh... no, nothing like that. I was just... you know, working fast. I didn't see anything that wasn't there.\n\nDoctor: During these recent weeks when you've been feeling so heavy and sad, have you had any thoughts that life is not worth living?\n\nPatient: I... yeah. I guess. \n\nUm... I just keep having these thoughts that... that my family, and my clients... maybe they'd just be better off if I wasn't around. I'm not going to do anything. I don't have a plan or whatever. It just... it scares me a little. How often it comes up in my head.\n\nDoctor: During these recent weeks when you've been feeling so heavy and sad, have you noticed any significant changes in your sleep patterns or your appetite?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I guess sleep's been... bad. \n\nEven if I go to bed at a normal time, I just... I toss around. And then I wake up way before dawn. I just lay there thinking about... things. Like this missed delivery I had. And when I finally drag myself up, I don't feel rested at all. \n\nAppetite's... fine, I guess. I don't know.\n\nDoctor: Have you found it difficult to perform your daily tasks, such as working at your jewelry bench or taking care of your basic needs, during these past few weeks of feeling so low?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I guess. It's... it's been hard to keep up. \n\nI actually... I missed a major custom delivery. And the records in my shop, they just... they got really disorganized. I had to tell people I can't take new work right now. \n\nAnd... my family's been coming around more. I guess they noticed I wasn't... doing my usual stuff. They got worried, so they keep checking on me. But yeah, it's been hard to get things done.\n\nDoctor: During these past few weeks when you have been feeling this way, have you been able to function at all, or have you been completely unable to carry out your work or daily responsibilities?\n\nPatient: I mean... I'm not completely unable to do anything. I can still get out of bed eventually and... eat, I guess. But work is just... I told you, I'm not taking new orders right now. I just can't focus on it.\n\nDoctor: During these past few weeks when you've been feeling so low, have you noticed that your movements or your speech have slowed down to the point where others might have commented on it?\n\nPatient: No... not really. I don't think so. My family hasn't said anything about how I talk or move... I just don't have a lot of energy, that's all.\n\nDoctor: Over these past few weeks when you've been feeling low, have you lost interest or pleasure in almost all of the activities you usually enjoy, including things outside of your jewelry work?\n\nPatient: I don't... no, not really. I still do some things. Just... regular stuff, I guess. I haven't really thought about it.\n\nDoctor: During these past few weeks, have you felt so tired or drained of energy that you feel like you have almost no energy to do anything at all?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I guess. I just... I'm really tired. When I try to sit at my bench, my arms just feel heavy... like lead, I guess. And if I try to polish a setting or... or just move a tray of stones, it just takes everything out of me. Even if I sleep, I still wake up feeling just as wiped out. It's just... hard to do much.\n\nDoctor: Even if you are still doing some regular things, have you found that you no longer get any real enjoyment or pleasure out of the hobbies or activities you used to love?\n\nPatient: I don't know... I guess I still like things okay. It's just... I'm tired, like I said. I haven't really noticed not liking things.\n\nDoctor: When you do engage in those regular activities you mentioned, do you find that you are still able to experience a sense of joy or pleasure from them, or does it feel more like you are just going through the motions?\n\nPatient: I mean... I guess I just do them. It's not like I'm jumping for joy, but... I don't know. I just do what I need to do. I haven't really paid attention to how it feels.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing.
